# Ancestral Manifestations Registry

Status: Working, limited high-confidence first set

These manifestations translate several Gifts into modern Hollow Grove design. They are proposals and references, not newly established offices, districts, gameplay systems, or permissions. Historical form must pass through the existing geography, materials, palettes, technology, and constitutional authority.

| House | Count |
|---|---:|
| Stonebend | 41 |
| Sandmanor | 41 |
| Glaüshouse | 41 |
| Flynt | 41 |

# Stonebend

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.foundation_archive"></a>
## Foundation Archive

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.foundation_archive`<br>
**Manifestation:** Foundation Archive<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Keep the identity, authority, dependencies, materials, alterations, and maintenance obligations of every major Stonebend work publicly traceable.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Sumerian administrative tablets and sealings, managed canal dependencies, and city-scale institutional organization. See the [Sumer dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_01/SUMER.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.sumer.administrative_recordkeeping`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.sumer.administrative_recordkeeping), [`gift.stonebend.sumer.irrigation_and_canal_governance`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.sumer.irrigation_and_canal_governance), [`gift.stonebend.sumer.urban_institutional_planning`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.sumer.urban_institutional_planning)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend converts early record practice into constitutional provenance: an archive supports Identity and maintenance but never grants Claim, Title, or Yield by itself.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue civic interior with Stonebend materials, stepped reading levels, durable indexed surfaces, visible service diagrams, and foundation inscriptions; no literal ziggurat, cuneiform façade, or Mesopotamian costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A documentation service inside existing Stonebend public-record and works authority, not a new office or court.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only building provenance, inspection context, repair history, archival research, and future restoration-quest hooks.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Reference `manifestation.stonebend.foundation_archive`; display source Gifts and current constitutional owner separately; require later runtime ownership before any state mutation.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** civic archivist<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** artifact provenance, building inspection, and maintenance-history references<br>
**Confidence:** High for recordkeeping and urban institutions; medium for any single archive form across the full Point range.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/the-origins-of-writing); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://cdli.earth/P003801); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.cambridge.org/highereducation/books/sumer-and-the-sumerians/5FEFFC85BF6EC0DA895702207C8741A1)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:1 — Birth of Cities](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_01_birth_of_cities.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.territorial_stewardship_registry"></a>
## Territorial Stewardship Registry

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.territorial_stewardship_registry`<br>
**Manifestation:** Territorial Stewardship Registry<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Make the delegated scope, local custodian, reporting path, service dependencies, and maintenance responsibility of distributed Stonebend works publicly traceable.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Old Akkadian governors and local institutions, Akkadian and Sumerian scribal continuity, messenger reporting, and long-range administrative coordination. See the [Akkad dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_02/AKKAD.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.akkad.delegated_territorial_administration`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.akkad.delegated_territorial_administration), [`gift.stonebend.akkad.bilingual_scribal_continuity`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.akkad.bilingual_scribal_continuity), [`gift.stonebend.akkad.long_range_institutional_coordination`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.akkad.long_range_institutional_coordination)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend translates layered territorial administration into bounded public stewardship; the registry identifies authority but cannot create Claim, Title, Yield, or central supremacy.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue civic interface and records hall with layered jurisdiction maps, parallel-language records, service diagrams, change histories, and local custody markers; no palace, victory stele, or imperial insignia is copied.<br>
**Institutional role:** A documentation and coordination service within existing Stonebend public-record and works authority, not a governorate or new ministry.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only jurisdiction lookup, public-work provenance, maintenance dependencies, translated record comparison, and future repair or inspection context.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Keep current constitutional owner, historical source, and translation version as separate fields; never infer authority from geographic scale or an ancestral tag.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** regional records steward<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** jurisdiction lookup, works provenance, service handoff, and maintenance context<br>
**Confidence:** High for layered administration, bilingual scribal practice, and reporting; medium for the uniformity of control across all claimed Akkadian territory.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/the-akkadian-period-ca-2350-2150-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://cdli.earth/cdli-tablet/345); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/etcsri/Q000834)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:2 — First Empires](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_02_first_empires.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.civic_obligation_ledger"></a>
## Civic Obligation Ledger

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.civic_obligation_ledger`<br>
**Manifestation:** Civic Obligation Ledger<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Make every measured civic obligation, delivery, work interval, responsible office, exception, and completion record inspectable across the life of a public work.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Ur III administrative accounting, provincial delivery and redistribution archives, dated legal records, labor documentation, and year-name chronology. See the [Ur III dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_03/UR_III.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.ur_iii.standardized_administrative_accounting`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.ur_iii.standardized_administrative_accounting), [`gift.stonebend.ur_iii.provincial_obligation_and_redistribution_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.ur_iii.provincial_obligation_and_redistribution_records), [`gift.stonebend.ur_iii.dated_legal_and_labor_documentation`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.ur_iii.dated_legal_and_labor_documentation)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives accountable comparison, dating, custody, and maintenance responsibility. It rejects coerced labor, royal command, and the premise that an entry in a ledger creates constitutional authority.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue records hall and distributed interface with Stonebend materials, calibrated surfaces, source-office marks, visible timelines, dependency maps, and amendment layers; no cuneiform wallpaper, royal statue, or copied ziggurat form.<br>
**Institutional role:** A record service inside existing Stonebend works, standards, and Title authority; it does not impose labor, assess tax, or create Claim, Title, or Yield.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only maintenance schedules, delivery provenance, work-history inspection, responsibility handoffs, exception review, and future bounded restoration context.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Keep ancient source type, archive, date formula, and coercion risk visible; separate historical accounting evidence from the modern office that owns a task.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** civic obligation registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** maintenance schedule, delivery provenance, responsibility handoff, and exception review<br>
**Confidence:** High for extensive administrative, dated, legal, and labor records; medium for treating the debated bala system as one uniform provincial mechanism.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.jstor.org/stable/10.3138/j.ctt13x1qkz); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://oi.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/saoc46.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/329067)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:3 — Restoration Kingdoms](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_03_restoration_kingdoms.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.case_and_measure_hall"></a>
## Case and Measure Hall

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.case_and_measure_hall`<br>
**Manifestation:** Case and Measure Hall<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Let residents compare jurisprudence, contracts, measures, worked problems, source limits, and present authority in one inspectable civic-learning setting.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Old Babylonian law collections, sealed legal and commercial records, scribal schools, sexagesimal place-value tables, and mathematical problem texts, with Babylon's changing political scope kept explicit. See the [Babylon dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_04/BABYLON.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.babylon.publicly_inscribed_jurisprudence`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.babylon.publicly_inscribed_jurisprudence), [`gift.stonebend.babylon.sealed_contract_and_case_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.babylon.sealed_contract_and_case_records), [`gift.stonebend.babylon.place_value_mathematical_problem_practice`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.babylon.place_value_mathematical_problem_practice)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives public reasoning, documentation, units, worked procedure, and correction. It does not receive Hammurabi's kingship, unequal ancient penalties, coerced labor, or a second source of law, Claim, Title, or Yield.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue civic interior with Stonebend materials, calibrated work surfaces, reversible case panels, contract and witness layers, source-object images, unit converters, worked diagrams, and visible corrections; no lawgiver throne, faux cuneiform wallpaper, or Babylon replica.<br>
**Institutional role:** A library, archive, and standards teaching program within existing Stonebend record, education, and Title boundaries; it cannot decide a case, certify a right, or promulgate law.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only case comparison, tablet provenance, unit and assumption inspection, curriculum references, translation history, error checking, and future bounded measurement or archive tasks.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Label the Hammurabi monument a royal law collection or jurisprudential monument rather than a modern code; show ancient social rank and coercion; attach provenience and genre to every contract and problem tablet.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** case-record curator<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** source comparison, measurement curriculum, and provenance display<br>
**Confidence:** High for the law stele, contracts, sealed records, and mathematical problem tradition; medium for reconstructing any one standardized school curriculum across the full Old Babylonian world.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.louvre.fr/en/the-code-of-hammurabi); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/the-isin-larsa-and-old-babylonian-periods-2004-1595-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/dccmt/); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1896-0402-69)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:4 — Old Babylonian World](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_04_old_babylonian_world.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.rupture_and_continuity_ledger"></a>
## Rupture-and-Continuity Ledger

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.rupture_and_continuity_ledger`<br>
**Manifestation:** Rupture-and-Continuity Ledger<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Keep state collapse, destroyed institutions, surviving communities, displaced records, new jurisdictions, and portable memory separately dated and attributable across Judah's 612–539 BCE transition.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Babylonian campaign chronicles, Judahite destruction and settlement archaeology, Lachish letters, ration tablets naming Jehoiachin, Babylonian legal records involving Judahites, and differently situated biblical literary witnesses. See the [Judah dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_04/JUDAH_KINGDOM_FALL_AND_COMMUNITIES.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.judah.multi_stage_state_rupture_chronology`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.judah.multi_stage_state_rupture_chronology), [`gift.stonebend.judah.distributed_community_and_household_legal_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.judah.distributed_community_and_household_legal_records), [`gift.stonebend.judah.portable_textual_and_institutional_memory`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.judah.portable_textual_and_institutional_memory)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives staged rupture analysis, community-and-record custody, legal-context attribution, and durable memory under institutional change. It does not receive Davidic succession, covenant, temple authority, deportation, imperial land service, or remembered territory as current Law, Claim, or Title.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue Stonebend records room with parallel timelines for capital, monarchy, temple, settlement, households, archives, and jurisdiction; movable custody paths; named source panes; surviving-service indicators; and explicit missing-data fields—no ruined-temple tableau, biblical pageant, royal seal enlargement, or exile spectacle.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-history, archive-continuity, and institutional-handoff service within existing Presynce, permanence, record, maintenance, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot establish ancestry, land right, doctrine, or succession.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only rupture chronology, community and document custody, institution-loss comparison, legal-context changes, curriculum, and future bounded archive-recovery or continuity references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Represent 609, 605, 597, and 587/586 separately; distinguish kingdom, province, homeland, Babylonia, and Egypt; identify deportation, migration, assigned land service, and return by source; attach literary witnesses to proposed layers and dates; never imply the whole population left.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** continuity provenance registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** rupture-state chronology, community and record custody, institutional handoff, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for the staged Babylonian interventions, 597 deportation, Jerusalem's destruction, Jehoiachin ration records, and continued Judahite communities; medium to contested for 587 versus 586, deportation scale, local administrative sequence, individual literary layers, and the exact geography of Al-Yahudu records.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://academic.oup.com/reference/62341/reference-article-abstract/554100061); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/34226/chapter-abstract/290215186); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://academic.oup.com/british-academy-scholarship-online/book/21132/chapter/180736890); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/neo/pager)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:4 — Neo-Babylonian World](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_04_neo_babylonian_world.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.relay_road_spine"></a>
## Relay and Jurisdiction Spine

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.relay_road_spine`<br>
**Manifestation:** Relay and Jurisdiction Spine<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Keep every public route segment, relay, delegated jurisdiction, language, travel authorization, ration or supply record, service dependency, and maintenance handoff traceable without reproducing imperial access.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Achaemenid regional government, royal and local institutions, the Persepolis Fortification and Treasury archives, multilingual royal and administrative records, inherited and imperial roads, travel authorizations, messengers, and ration-supported movement. See the [Achaemenid dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_05/ACHAEMENID_EMPIRE_539_336.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.delegated_regional_administration_with_local_jurisdiction_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.delegated_regional_administration_with_local_jurisdiction_records), [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.multilingual_imperial_document_provenance`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.multilingual_imperial_document_provenance), [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.road_relay_and_ration_supported_coordination`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.road_relay_and_ration_supported_coordination)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives delegated-scope provenance, multilingual record custody, route-and-relay coordination, and auditable service responsibility. It does not receive imperial sovereignty, satrapal privilege, taxation, tribute, coerced labor, suppression, conquest, or royal road access as Law, Claim, or Title.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue Stonebend transit and records spine with modular relay bays, local-jurisdiction maps, parallel-language record panes, route and ration provenance, service dependency markings, accessible maintenance corridors, and visible handoff dates; no Persepolis gate, Apadana procession, royal throne, pseudo-cuneiform décor, or Persian costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-route, archive, works, and jurisdiction-reference service within existing Law of Identity, Presynce, permanence, maintenance, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot grant movement, create an office, or override local authority.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only route provenance, jurisdiction and language lookup, relay and supply custody, maintenance-state comparison, archive curriculum, and future bounded travel or restoration references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Separate royal claim, regional office, local institution, language, legal forum, archive catchment, route segment, authorization, ration, labor status, and maintenance owner; do not treat `satrapy`, `Royal Road`, or trilingual inscription as uniform empire-wide fields; keep the 336–330 continuation visible.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** relay-jurisdiction registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** route provenance, jurisdiction and language lookup, supply custody, maintenance handoff, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for Achaemenid rule through 336, regional administration, major roads, Persepolis provisioning and travel records, and multilingual archives; medium for consistent empire-wide office hierarchy and relay procedure; contested where later Greek descriptions are generalized beyond their evidence.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/persepolis-admin-archive/); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/achaemenid-satrapies/); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://oi.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/oip92.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/achaemenid-royal-communication/); [Scholarly or collection source 5](https://www.metmuseum.org/de/essays/the-achaemenid-persian-empire-550-330-b-c)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:5 — Achaemenid World](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_05_achaemenid_world.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.terminal_sovereignty_register"></a>
## Terminal Sovereignty Register

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.terminal_sovereignty_register`<br>
**Manifestation:** Terminal Sovereignty Register<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Separate the end of a polity from the survival, reassignment, alteration, or loss of every office, jurisdiction, record set, route, public service, institution, and community affected by conquest.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Darius III's 336–330 reign, staggered Achaemenid military and political loss, regional resistance, Alexander's selective retention and replacement of officials and satrapal structures, and successor reuse of inherited jurisdictions. See the [Achaemenid dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_06/ACHAEMENID_END_AND_INSTITUTIONAL_CONTINUITY_336_281.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.terminal_polity_chronology_with_surviving_institutions`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.terminal_polity_chronology_with_surviving_institutions), [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.jurisdiction_handoff_across_conquest`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.jurisdiction_handoff_across_conquest), [`gift.stonebend.achaemenid.administrative_continuity_with_changed_sovereign_attribution`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.achaemenid.administrative_continuity_with_changed_sovereign_attribution)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives terminal-state chronology, explicit authority handoff, and changed-sovereign provenance. It does not receive conquest, succession by force, royal appropriation, administrative continuity, or institutional survival as Law, Claim, Title, or legitimacy.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue Stonebend records environment with separate rails for sovereign, capital, territory, office, local institution, archive, route, service, and community; each rail shows outgoing authority, incoming authority, interruption, custody, resistance, repair, and unresolved status—no burning-Persepolis tableau, royal diadem, battle map, or Achaemenid/Macedonian costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-history, jurisdiction, archive-continuity, and institutional-handoff reference inside existing Law of Identity, Presynce, permanence, maintenance, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot transfer authority or decide a present claim.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only terminal chronology, office and service handoff, archive custody, surviving-community comparison, source-confidence display, curriculum, and future bounded continuity or restoration references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Represent 336, 334, 333, 331, and 330 separately; distinguish battle, capital occupation, king's death, dynastic end, regional resistance, office reassignment, institutional continuity, and later successor allocation; preserve Greek-source imbalance and local evidence; never mark conquest as a clean migration or lawful transfer.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** sovereignty-transition registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** terminal-state chronology, authority handoff, service continuity, record custody, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for Darius III's 336–330 reign, principal campaign sequence, dynastic end, and substantial reuse of regional administration; medium for the exact continuity of individual offices and personnel; lower where later Greek narratives are the only detailed witness.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/darius-v/); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/achaemenid-satrapies/); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/alexander-the-great-356-23-bc/); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/chronology-of-iranian-history-part-1/); [Scholarly or collection source 5](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/gaugamela-/)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:6 — Alexander and the Diadochi](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_06_alexander_and_the_diadochi.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.multi_witness_state_register"></a>
## Multi-Witness State Register

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.multi_witness_state_register`<br>
**Manifestation:** Multi-Witness State Register<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Keep the northern kingdom's internal records, neighboring and imperial claims, material phases, later literary memory, terminal events, and present Atlas interpretation separately attributable and mutually comparable.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Samaria ostraca and architecture; contested construction phases at Samaria, Jezreel, Megiddo, and Hazor; the Mesha and Tel Dan monuments; Assyrian annals and images; and later Kings, Amos, and Hosea traditions with composition history attached. See the [Israel dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_02/ISRAEL_NORTHERN_KINGDOM.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.israel.multi_source_state_chronology`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.israel.multi_source_state_chronology), [`gift.stonebend.israel.administrative_collection_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.israel.administrative_collection_records), [`gift.stonebend.israel.attributed_urban_and_water_infrastructure`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.israel.attributed_urban_and_water_infrastructure)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives concordance, phase attribution, administrative provenance, infrastructure memory, and honest terminal records. It does not receive royal territorial Claim, biblical covenant as constitutional law, elite extraction, a settled construction chronology, or Title from ancient rule.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern dark-blue Stonebend record room with parallel source lanes, movable reign and phase bands, delivery-place maps, sectioned water and building diagrams, destruction and reuse layers, confidence markers, and an explicit terminal-attribution panel; no Israelite palace replica, biblical tableau, tribal emblem system, or royal throne.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-history, archive, and works-provenance service within existing Stonebend Presynce, record, maintenance, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot establish ancestry, territory, doctrine, or legal succession.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only source concordance, ostracon and object provenance, phase attribution, infrastructure maintenance history, terminal-event comparison, curriculum, and future bounded archival investigation references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Label `Israel` here as the northern kingdom; keep Judah separate; attach date and speaker to every inscription; distinguish commodity delivery from a reconstructed tax system; and show Shalmaneser V, Sargon II, biblical, chronicle, and archaeological terminal evidence without forcing one unqualified account.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** state-record concordance curator<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** source concordance, phase attribution, infrastructure provenance, terminal-state audit, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for Samaria as the ninth-century capital, the independent inscriptional attestations, the early eighth-century ostraca, 732 territorial annexation, and the kingdom's terminal conquest; medium to contested for individual building attributions, exact fiscal reconstruction, and the 722/720 sequence.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://isac.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/oimp31.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.asor.org/news/2019/03/nea82_1-toc/); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://academic.oup.com/reference/62341/reference-article-abstract/554097469); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark:/53355/cl010120339); [Scholarly or collection source 5](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/saao/aebp/essentials/countries/israel/)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:2 — Kings, Prophets, and Assyrian Expansion](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_02_kings_prophets_and_assyrian_expansion.md)

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.public_works_academy"></a>
## Successive Works Academy

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.public_works_academy`<br>
**Manifestation:** Successive Works Academy<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Teach how durable public works are conceived, drafted, attributed, executed, corrected, maintained, and reinterpreted across more than one administration or generation.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** New Kingdom temple additions across successive reigns, foundation and building inscriptions, Deir el-Medina work-community records, figured ostraca, drafts, revisions, and specialist craft practice. See the [New Kingdom Egypt dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_05/NEW_KINGDOM_EGYPT.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.successive_reign_building_attribution`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.successive_reign_building_attribution), [`gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.specialist_work_community_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.specialist_work_community_records), [`gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.drafting_and_correction_practice`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.new_kingdom_egypt.drafting_and_correction_practice)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives phase attribution, skilled-work memory, visible revision, and continuing maintenance. It does not receive pharaonic sovereignty, monumental excess, coerced labor, erased predecessors, or a new source of Claim, Title, or Yield.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue Stonebend teaching and archive space with full-scale work surfaces, layered phase diagrams, material samples, visible corrections, named team records, maintenance seams, and reversible exhibits; no pylon, tomb, obelisk, hieroglyphic wallpaper, or pharaonic costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-works, archive, and professional-education program within existing Stonebend Freemason, Proliteriate, standards, record, and Title boundaries; it cannot commission a work or confer office.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only phase provenance, draft comparison, correction history, crew and supply context, maintenance records, curriculum, and future bounded inspection or restoration references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Separate original commission, work community, ancient authority, later alteration, modern interpretation, and current constitutional custodian; expose erased or displaced attribution and coercive labor context.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** works record instructor<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** phase provenance, draft comparison, maintenance history, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for successive monumental additions and dense Deir el-Medina records; high for sketches and corrections, but medium for generalizing one exceptional royal work community to all New Kingdom building.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/egypt-in-the-new-kingdom-ca-1550-1070-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/deirelmedine/ostraca.html); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/ostraka/figured.html); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://www.metmuseum.org/met-publications/hatshepsut-from-queen-to-pharaoh)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:5 — Great Bronze Age Powers](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_05_great_bronze_age_powers.md)

## Layered Foundations Register

**Manifestation:** Layered Foundations Register<br>

**Purpose:** Keep settlement phase, household infrastructure, external attestation, later foundation narrative, monarchic claim, scholarly reconstruction, and present constitutional authority separately traceable.<br>

See the [Kingdom of Israel dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_01/KINGDOM_OF_ISRAEL.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.clustered_highland_settlement_networks`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.clustered_highland_settlement_networks), [`gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.household_agrarian_storage_and_water_practice`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.household_agrarian_storage_and_water_practice), [`gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.layered_preservation_of_foundation_and_kingship_traditions`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.kingdom_of_israel.layered_preservation_of_foundation_and_kingship_traditions)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives layered foundation provenance, settlement-scale coordination, and material continuity. It does not receive biblical kingship, covenant as constitutional law, a territorial claim, proof of a united monarchy, or Title from later memory.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern dark-blue Stonebend archive and planning room with terrain and settlement layers, household service diagrams, object and inscription witnesses, movable date bands, competing reconstruction overlays, and conspicuous uncertainty gaps; no biblical tableau, Iron Age village replica, temple reconstruction, or royal costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-history and infrastructure-provenance service within existing Stonebend archive, planning, Presynce, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot determine ancestry, grant land, or certify a historical narrative.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only settlement-phase comparison, source-type filtering, water and storage provenance, competing chronology display, curriculum, and future bounded archaeological or maintenance references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Never merge Merneptah's external name, an excavated settlement, a disputed early inscription, a later biblical narrative, and a modern map into one proof layer; Samaria and secure ninth-century royal evidence remain in the next Point.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** foundation evidence registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** settlement-phase comparison, source-status display, infrastructure provenance, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for the Merneptah reference and broad Iron I highland settlement increase; medium for household-pattern interpretation; low to contested for the scale, unity, and institutions of tenth-century monarchy.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.asor.org/news/2019/03/nea82_1-toc/); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://isac.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/oimp31.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://oi.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/saoc42_2ed.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://academic.oup.com/book/3360)<br>

<a id="manifestation.stonebend.layered_authority_mint_registry"></a>
## Layered Authority and Mint Registry

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.stonebend.layered_authority_mint_registry`<br>
**Manifestation:** Layered Authority and Mint Registry<br>
**Primary House:** [Stonebend](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Keep dynasty, reign, royal office, city, league, garrison, mint, district, rival claim, and post-monarchy settlement separately dated and attributable across the Antigonid coordinate.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Antigonus II's durable Macedonian rule from 277/276; successive Antigonid reigns; civic, league and garrison relationships; dynastic coinage with attribution problems; Pydna and abolition in 168; Roman district settlement; Andriscus and reorganization before 146. See the [Antigonid dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_07/ANTIGONID_MACEDONIA_AND_POST_MONARCHY_TRANSITION_281_146.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.stonebend.antigonid.phased_dynastic_and_post_dynastic_state_chronology`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.antigonid.phased_dynastic_and_post_dynastic_state_chronology), [`gift.stonebend.antigonid.royal_civic_league_and_garrison_jurisdiction_records`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.antigonid.royal_civic_league_and_garrison_jurisdiction_records), [`gift.stonebend.antigonid.mint_issue_and_monetary_authority_provenance`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.stonebend.antigonid.mint_issue_and_monetary_authority_provenance)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Stonebend receives phased state chronology, overlapping jurisdiction records, monetary-authority provenance, and explicit institutional handoff. It does not receive military kingship, garrison control, conquest, mint output, dynastic continuity, or Roman victory as Law, permanence, Claim, Title, or legitimacy.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary dark-blue Stonebend records hall with separate crown, civic, federal, garrison, mint, and district rails; coin-scale issue records; source-confidence and supersession fields; visible 277/276, 168, and 148/146 handoffs; no palace replica, Macedonian shield motif, royal portrait enlargement, trophy display, or antique mint theater.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public archive, standards, monetary-provenance, and jurisdiction-history service inside existing Law of Identity, Presynce, permanence, and Claim / Title / Yield boundaries; it cannot validate currency, transfer sovereignty, or decide a present claim.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only phase and office comparison, civic and league jurisdiction map, coin and mint provenance, garrison and district handoff, source-confidence display, curriculum, and future bounded archive references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Do not start durable Antigonid rule in 281 or extend kingship past 168; store ruler, issuing authority, mint, type, date range, find context, city, league, garrison, district, conflict, supersession, and uncertainty separately; distinguish currency circulation from territory and institutional survival from sovereign continuity.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** authority-and-mint provenance registrar<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** phase chronology, jurisdiction comparison, mint provenance, institutional handoff, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for Antigonus II's 277/276–239 reign, dynastic sequence, Pydna and abolition in 168; high for major coin series and sites; medium for specific crown-city-league jurisdiction and mint attribution; contested where later narratives or hoards carry most of the reconstruction.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/cambridge-ancient-history/macedonia-and-the-greek-leagues/4C9BFBE2FF478FCDFB6A254B0F0770F9); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/rome-and-the-third-macedonian-war/29BFEC976C80655FFDA300BA9EE742CF); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://discovery.ucl.ac.uk/1349335/); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://resources.metmuseum.org/resources/metpublications/pdf/Pergamon_and_the_Hellenistic_Kingdoms_of_the_Ancient_World.pdf); [Scholarly or collection source 5](https://coins.lib.virginia.edu/id/1990.18.6)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:7 — Mature Hellenistic World](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_07_mature_hellenistic_world.md)

**Current canon connection:** Law of Identity; permanence; Presynce; Diamond and Hypergiant; Freemason; Proliteriate; Claim / Title / Yield; see [Stonebend canon links](houses/STONEBEND_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** provenance and evidence-boundary steward<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** source date, claim scope, uncertainty, custody, affected communities, refusal, and revision references<br>
**Confidence:** Completion-level review: high confidence in the Point boundary and institutional source bundle; culture-specific detail remains revision-capable. Time-sensitive claims are current only through 2026-08-12 and must be re-reviewed.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://hai.stanford.edu/ai-index); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/european-approach-artificial-intelligence); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://investor.tsmc.com/static/annualReports/2025/english/index.html)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [VI:5 — AI Age](ages/age_06_information_civilization/point_05_ai_age.md)

# Sandmanor

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.ceremonial_garden_court"></a>
## Ceremonial Garden Court

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.ceremonial_garden_court`<br>
**Manifestation:** Ceremonial Garden Court<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Give Sandmanor a reviewable setting for public presentation, commemoration, testimony, and negotiated identity.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Early Dynastic Egyptian titulary, emblematic regalia, ordered ceremonial imagery, and courtly commemorative practice. See the [Early Dynastic Egypt dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_01/EARLY_DYNASTIC_EGYPT.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.royal_titulary_and_emblematic_identity`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.royal_titulary_and_emblematic_identity), [`gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.ceremonial_image_language`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.ceremonial_image_language), [`gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.court_ritual_and_commemorative_practice`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.early_dynastic_egypt.court_ritual_and_commemorative_practice)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** The court studies how identity is staged and remembered; it does not reproduce pharaonic sovereignty or create a new judicial authority.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern planted court in Sandmanor's locked palette with shaded processional choices, abstract name and emblem bands, water and textile surfaces, and reversible display frames; the garden is a Hollow Grove translation, not a claim about Early Dynastic garden archaeology.<br>
**Institutional role:** A programmable public space attached to an existing library, performance, festival, or Court precinct and governed by the responsible canon owner.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Cultural-memory exhibits, provenance-aware ceremony staging, testimony presentation, and future identity or diplomacy curriculum references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Avoid crowns, hieroglyphic pastiche, pyramids, and ruler cult; every emblem must identify its modern author, event, and bounded role.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** ceremonial interpreter<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** cultural-memory exhibit, hearing presentation, and provenance-aware event reference<br>
**Confidence:** High for emblematic identity and ceremonial image grammar; medium for the garden-form adaptation.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/archaicegypt/info.html); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://discovery.ucl.ac.uk/id/eprint/1445014/); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/Y_EA35714)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:1 — Birth of Cities](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_01_birth_of_cities.md)

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.continuity_memorial_court"></a>
## Continuity Memorial Court

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.continuity_memorial_court`<br>
**Manifestation:** Continuity Memorial Court<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Preserve attributed lives, service, loss, and changing public interpretation in a setting where commemoration can be examined rather than imposed.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Late Old Kingdom Pyramid Texts, integrated image-and-inscription programs, and provincial elite service biographies such as Weni and Harkhuf. See the [Old Kingdom Egypt dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_02/OLD_KINGDOM_EGYPT.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.inscribed_mortuary_remembrance`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.inscribed_mortuary_remembrance), [`gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.integrated_image_and_text_commemoration`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.integrated_image_and_text_commemoration), [`gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.provincial_service_biography`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.old_kingdom_egypt.provincial_service_biography)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Sandmanor receives methods for staging identity and remembrance, not royal divinity, funerary efficacy, social rank, or a new Court jurisdiction.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern shaded court of dark-blue foundations, Sandmanor textiles, planted thresholds, reversible image-and-text panels, recorded voices, and clearly attributed revisions; no pyramid, tomb chamber, hieroglyphic pastiche, or pharaonic costume.<br>
**Institutional role:** A memorial, library, performance, or Court-adjacent program governed by existing cultural and restitution authority.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Biographical archive browsing, source comparison, commemorative exhibit staging, public-memory curriculum, and future testimony context.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Mark elite self-presentation, later reconstruction, modern interpretation, and Hollow Grove adaptation separately; do not present tomb biography as representative of all Old Kingdom lives.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** memory interpreter<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** biographical archive, commemorative exhibit, curriculum, and testimony context<br>
**Confidence:** High for inscribed commemoration and image-text integration; medium for generalizing elite service biography beyond its surviving social context.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/egypt-in-the-old-kingdom-ca-2649-2150-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-collections/petrie-museum/highlights/pyramid-text); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/community-and-identity-in-ancient-egypt/9FBFB062DB70F28FA4B5DAF60FE51844)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:2 — First Empires](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_02_first_empires.md)

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.return_story_court"></a>
## Return Story Court

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.return_story_court`<br>
**Manifestation:** Return Story Court<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Provide an accountable setting for reading, staging, and comparing stories of fracture, loyalty, displacement, cultural encounter, reunion, and return.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Early Middle Kingdom narratives of restored unity, Theban and northern artistic synthesis, estate correspondence, and the manuscript tradition of Sinuhe. See the [Middle Kingdom Egypt dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_03/MIDDLE_KINGDOM_EGYPT.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.reunification_narrative_and_renewed_kingship`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.reunification_narrative_and_renewed_kingship), [`gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.regional_artistic_synthesis`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.regional_artistic_synthesis), [`gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.literature_of_loyalty_exile_and_return`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.middle_kingdom_egypt.literature_of_loyalty_exile_and_return)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Sandmanor receives a practice of examining how return and belonging are narrated. It does not reproduce kingship, impose loyalty, or turn one literary work into a universal Egyptian psychology.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern planted reading court in the locked Sandmanor palette with several entrances, reversible stage elements, paired regional materials, manuscript-date bands, attributed translations, and quiet correspondence alcoves; no pharaonic throne room or costume district.<br>
**Institutional role:** A library and performance program under existing Sandmanor cultural and Court boundaries; it creates no new hearing, judgment, reconciliation, or restitution authority.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only manuscript layers, translation comparison, performance provenance, curriculum, memory exhibits, and future dialogue context about home and return.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Display composition date, surviving witness date, fictional speaker, translator, and interpretation separately; preserve the Point's emergent Middle Kingdom date fit.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** return-story curator<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** layered text display, curriculum, performance provenance, and reconciliation dialogue context<br>
**Confidence:** High for reunification and regional artistic synthesis; high for the Sinuhe manuscript tradition, but medium for precise composition dates and royal commissioning claims.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/egypt-in-the-middle-kingdom-2030-1640-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/literature/sanehat/sources.html); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/545447)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [I:3 — Restoration Kingdoms](ages/age_01_ancient_civilizations/point_03_restoration_kingdoms.md)

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.harbor_diplomacy_school"></a>
## Harbor Mediation School

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.harbor_diplomacy_school`<br>
**Manifestation:** Harbor Mediation School<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Teach how independent coastal communities exchange language, objects, hospitality, technique, and negotiated identity while preserving who made, named, adapted, requested, and interpreted each thing.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** The distinct city histories of Byblos, Sidon, Tyre, Arwad, and neighboring ports; early Phoenician inscriptions; inherited alphabetic practice; maritime exchange; and craft combining Levantine, Egyptian, Cypriot, Aegean, and Syrian forms. See the [Phoenicia dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_01/PHOENICIA.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.independent_port_city_identity_and_exchange`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.independent_port_city_identity_and_exchange), [`gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.consonantal_alphabetic_inscription_and_transmission`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.consonantal_alphabetic_inscription_and_transmission), [`gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.cross_cultural_craft_synthesis`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.phoenicia.cross_cultural_craft_synthesis)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Sandmanor receives relationship-aware literacy, port-to-port mediation, local identity, and accountable synthesis. It does not receive a unified Phoenician nation, merchant entitlement, cultural anonymity, colonial romance, or a new Court, Proof, or recognition authority.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A contemporary planted coastal learning space with several harbor-facing entries, parallel sign-and-sound tables, object-biography stations, maker and route labels, translation rooms, and exchange maps in Sandmanor's locked palette; no cedar palace replica, purple-costume pageant, ship-themed décor, or generic Mediterranean bazaar.<br>
**Institutional role:** A language, design, hospitality, and provenance curriculum inside existing Sandmanor library, Minoan, Minorian, and Court-adjacent educational boundaries; it cannot negotiate or recognize an agreement.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only port relationship maps, inscription comparison, craft provenance, translation layers, curriculum, and future bounded dialogue or artifact context.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Use city and date before the umbrella term; distinguish language, script, artifact style, workshop, patron, route, and later findspot; record the debated date of the Ahiram inscription and never call Phoenician the first alphabet.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** harbor provenance mediator<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** port-to-port relationship maps, inscription comparison, object biography, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for independent coastal cities, long continuity, maritime exchange, craft synthesis, and Phoenician alphabetic transmission; medium for specific eleventh- or tenth-century diplomatic institutions and individual inscription dates.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/the-phoenicians-1500-300-b-c); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/295/); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://deaps.osu.edu/text_objects/11441); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://resources.metmuseum.org/resources/metpublications/pdf/Assyria_to_Iberia_at_the_Dawn_of_the_Classical_Age.pdf)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:1 — Early Iron Age](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_01_early_iron_age.md)

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.many_lives_gallery"></a>
## Many Lives Gallery

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.many_lives_gallery`<br>
**Manifestation:** Many Lives Gallery<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Present attributed people, kin and non-kin relationships, literary speakers, social roles, omissions, and changing interpretations without forcing them into one exemplary life.<br>

See the [Middle Kingdom Egypt dossier](dossiers/age_01_point_04/MIDDLE_KINGDOM_EGYPT_LATER_PHASE.md).<br>

**Modern House interpretation:** Sandmanor receives methods for relational memory and source-aware social reading. It does not reproduce funerary efficacy, royal ideology, elite rank, compulsory family structure, or a new hearing, Proof, or restitution process.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A modern planted gallery in the locked Sandmanor palette with grouped but revisable portraits, relationship lines, speaker and manuscript labels, several reading paths, quiet listening alcoves, and visible gaps; no tomb chapel, pharaonic costume, hieroglyphic pastiche, or cult replica.<br>
**Institutional role:** A public-memory, library, and interpretation program under existing Sandmanor cultural and Court-adjacent boundaries; it creates no legal finding or recognized status.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only relationship maps, source and manuscript layers, translation comparison, memory exhibits, curriculum, and future dialogue context about role, voice, and omission.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Separate historical person, represented role, kinship claim, literary speaker, manuscript witness, translator, and modern interpretation; state that the surviving memorial corpus is socially selective.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** attribution curator<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** memory exhibits, source-layer comparison, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for expanded private commemoration and named relationship groups; high for the existence of varied Middle Kingdom literary and Lahun records; medium for precise composition dates and broad social representativeness.<br>

<a id="manifestation.sandmanor.reciprocal_design_salon"></a>
## Many-Polis Interpretation Theater

**Manifestation ID:** `manifestation.sandmanor.reciprocal_design_salon`<br>
**Manifestation:** Many-Polis Interpretation Theater<br>
**Primary House:** [Sandmanor](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md)<br>
**Purpose:** Let creators compare how different Greek poleis recorded argument, staged inherited stories, formed coalitions, excluded participants, and revised public relationships without turning Athens into all Greece.<br>
**Historical ancestry:** Polis-specific decrees, laws, treaties and civic spaces; Athenian Agora records with explicit evidence bias; Dionysiac festival tragedy and comedy; shifting Delian, Peloponnesian, Boeotian, and fourth-century coalitions; and civic systems that included citizens while excluding many residents. See the [Greek dossier](dossiers/age_02_point_05/GREEK_WORLD_539_336.md).<br>
**Source Gifts:** [`gift.sandmanor.greek.polis_specific_civic_argument_and_public_record`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.greek.polis_specific_civic_argument_and_public_record), [`gift.sandmanor.greek.festival_theater_as_civic_interpretation`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.greek.festival_theater_as_civic_interpretation), [`gift.sandmanor.greek.inter_polis_diplomacy_and_shifting_coalition_identity`](ANCESTRAL_GIFTS_REGISTRY.md#gift.sandmanor.greek.inter_polis_diplomacy_and_shifting_coalition_identity)<br>
**Modern House interpretation:** Sandmanor receives accountable civic argument, staged interpretation, public record, coalition memory, and visible revision. It does not receive universal democracy, citizenship exclusion, slavery, allied subordination, colonial entitlement, sacred festival authority, or performance as consent or Proof.<br>
**Physical appearance:** A planted dark-blue Sandmanor theater and forum with several nonhierarchical approaches, polity-specific record panels, a reversible central stage, audience-and-exclusion maps, chorus and sponsor provenance, treaty layers, and coalition reconfiguration paths; no Acropolis replica, marble polis set, masks as ethnic costume, or generic Greek ornament.<br>
**Institutional role:** A library, theater, diplomacy, and design-education program inside existing Law of Pattern, Prefog, Prefig, Minorian, Minoan, Contest of Improvement, Court-adjacent, and Restitution boundaries; it cannot judge identity, decide a case, or create ritual authority.<br>
**Gameplay potential:** Read-only polity comparison, proposal and counterclaim display, festival-text provenance, audience and exclusion audit, coalition relationship maps, curriculum, and future bounded performance or diplomacy references.<br>
**Implementation notes:** Store polity, date, constitution type, eligible participant, excluded population, institution, record genre, speaker, sponsor, festival, story source, obligation, asymmetry, dissent, and later reception; keep Athens's dense evidence visibly nonrepresentative; separate alliance from empire and drama from direct democratic transcript.<br>
**Current canon connection:** Law of Pattern; Prefog; Prefig; Minorians; Minoans; Contest of Improvement; Court and Restitution; see [Sandmanor canon links](houses/SANDMANOR_ANCESTRY.md#existing-canon-bridge).<br>
**Possible NPC role:** civic interpretation dramaturg<br>
**Possible Hueman data hook:** polity-specific record comparison, staged interpretation, coalition relationship maps, exclusion audit, and curriculum references<br>
**Confidence:** High for Greek political plurality, Athenian civic records, fifth-century festival drama, and major coalition changes; medium for reconstructing audience response and non-Athenian institutions from uneven evidence; low wherever a pan-Greek practice is inferred from Athens alone.<br>
**Sources:** [Scholarly or collection source 1](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/excavations/athenian-agora?v=default); [Scholarly or collection source 2](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/publications/books/open-access-books/athenian-agora-oa); [Scholarly or collection source 3](https://www.atticinscriptions.com/papers/aio-papers-10/); [Scholarly or collection source 4](https://www.metmuseum.org/pt/essays/theater-in-ancient-greece); [Scholarly or collection source 5](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/archaic-and-classical-greece/athens-6373/5732B68184236E4BB824334474D2F7B3)<br>
**Status:** Reviewed flagship; design remains Working until separately adopted<br>
**Ancestral provenance:** [II:5 — Achaemenid World](ages/age_02_classical_civilization/point_05_achaemenid_world.md)
